Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the tumors space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 601 and the convergence rate is O(56/n^5).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 601 + O(1/n^56). Summing over all modes and applying the tumors convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)
